0, error: 0 };\n127\t\n128\t    for (const worker of workers) {\n129\t      if (worker.status === 'active') stats.active++;\n130\t      else if (worker.status === 'idle') stats.idle++;\n131\t Activity Monitor';\n145\t\n146\t    // Add filter indicator if CLI filters are active\n147\t    if (this.options.filter?.worker || this.options.filter?.level) {\n148\t      const filterParts: string[] this.deletePresetByName(presetName);\n686\t    } else if (cmd === 'preset:save') {\n687\t      this.showPresetSaveDialog();\n688\t    } else if (cmd === 'preset:list') {\n689\t      const presets = * Parse duration string (e.g., \"5m\", \"1h\", \"30s\") to milliseconds\n864\t   */\n865\t  private parseDuration(duration: string): number | null {\n866\t    const match = }\n878\t  }\n879\t\n880\t  /**\n881\t   * Handle time filter command (e.g., \"5m\", \"1h\", \"30s\")\n882\t   */\n883\t  private handleTimeFilter(duration: string): void {\n884\t    const ms = this.updateBudgetPanel();\n      },\n    });\n    this.budgetAlertPanel.hide();\n\n    // Footer with key hints\n    this.footerBox = blessed.box({\n      parent: this.screen,\n      tags: true,\n      bottom: 0,\n      left: 0,\n      right: 0,\n      height: 1,\n      content: this.getFooterContent(),\n      style: {\n        fg: colors.muted,\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get footer content based on current state\n   */\n  private getFooterContent(): string {\n    if (this.viewMode === 'default') {\n      let content = ' [Tab] Switch  [j/k] Scroll  [/] Search  [H] Heatmap  [D] DAG  [E] Errors  [I] Git  [C] Collisions  [N] Narrative  [A] Analytics  [B] Budget  [T] Transcript  [X] XRef';\n\n      // Show focus mode status\n      if (this.focusModeEnabled) {\n        content += '  {green-fg}[FOCUS MODE]{/}';\n        if (this.pinnedWorkerId) {\n          content += ` Worker:${this.pinnedWorkerId.slice(0, 8)}`;\n        }\n        if (this.pinnedBeadId) {\n          content += ` Bead:${this.pinnedBeadId}`;\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Show file context status\n      if (this.fileContextVisible) {\n        content += `  {cyan-fg}[FILE CONTEXT ${Math.round((1-this.splitRatio)*100)}%]{/}`;\n      }\n\n      // Show current theme\n      content += `  {${this.currentTheme === 'dark' ? 'blue' : 'yellow'}-fg}[${this.currentTheme.toUpperCase()}]{/}`;\n\n      content += '  [p]Pin Worker  [P]Pin Bead  [F]Focus';\n      content += '  [Ctrl+F]File Panel  [{/}]Resize';\n      content += '  [[/]]Focus Presets';\n      content += '  [Ctrl+T]Theme  [?] Help  [q] Quit';\n\n      return content;\n    }\n\n    // Return default content for other views\n    return ' [Tab] Switch  [j/k] Scroll  [/] Search  [H] Heatmap  [D] DAG  [E] Errors  [C] Collisions  [N] Narrative  [A] Analytics  [B] Budget  [T] Transcript  [X] XRef  [?] Help  [q] Quit';\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Bind keyboard shortcuts\n   */\n  private bindKeys(): void {\n    // Quit\n    this.screen.key(['q', 'C-c'], () => {\n      this.stop();\n    });\n\n    // Help toggle\n    this.screen.key(['?'], () => {\n      this.toggleHelp();\n    });\n\n    // Tab switching\n    this.screen.key(['tab'], () => {\n      this.screen.focusNext();\n    });\n\n    this.screen.key(['S-tab'], () => {\n      this.screen.focusPrevious();\n    });\n\n    // Refresh\n    this.screen.key(['r'], () => {\n      this.render();\n    });\n\n    // Command palette\n    this.screen.key(['C-k'], () => {\n      // Update command palette with current presets before showing\n      this.updateCommandPalettePresets();\n      this.commandPalette.toggle();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle worker detail\n    this.screen.key(['enter'], () => {\n      const selected = this.workerGrid.getSelected();\n      if (selected) {\n        this.showWorkerDetail(selected);\n      }\n    });\n\n    // Toggle file heatmap view\n    this.screen.key(['H', 'h'], () => {\n      this.toggleHeatmapView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle dependency DAG view\n    this.screen.key(['D', 'd'], () => {\n      this.toggleDagView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle session replay view\n    this.screen.key(['R', 'r'], () => {\n      this.toggleReplayView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle error group view\n    this.screen.key(['E', 'e'], () => {\n      this.toggleErrorsView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle session digest view\n    this.screen.key(['G', 'g'], () => {\n      this.toggleDigestView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle collision alert view\n    this.screen.key(['C', 'c'], () => {\n      this.toggleCollisionsView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle git integration view\n    this.screen.key(['I'], () => {\n      this.toggleGitView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle semantic narrative view\n    this.screen.key(['N'], () => {\n      this.toggleNarrativeView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle worker analytics view\n    this.screen.key(['A'], () => {\n      this.toggleAnalyticsView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle budget dashboard view\n    this.screen.key(['B'], () => {\n      this.toggleBudgetView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle conversation transcript view\n    this.screen.key(['T'], () => {\n      this.toggleTranscriptView();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle cross-reference view\n    this.screen.key(['X'], () => {\n      this.toggleXrefView();\n    });\n\n    // Escape to close modals or return to default view\n    this.screen.key(['escape'], () => {\n      // First, hide worker detail if visible\n      if (this.workerDetail.isVisible()) {\n        this.workerDetail.hide();\n        return;\n      }\n      // Then, return to default view if in another view\n      if (this.viewMode !== 'default') {\n        this.setViewMode('default');\n      }\n    });\n\n    // Focus mode keybindings\n    this.screen.key(['p'], () => {\n      this.toggleWorkerPin();\n    });\n\n    this.screen.key(['P'], () => {\n      this.toggleBeadPin();\n    });\n\n    this.screen.key(['F'], () => {\n      this.toggleFocusMode();\n    });\n\n    // Toggle file context panel (Ctrl+F)\n    this.screen.key(['C-f'], () => {\n      this.toggleFileContextPanel();\n    });\n\n    // Resize split view (Shift+[ and Shift+])\n    this.screen.key(['{'], () => {\n      this.resizeFileContext(-0.05);\n    });\n\n    this.screen.key(['}'], () => {\n      this.resizeFileContext(0.05);\n    });\n\n    // Focus preset save/load\n    this.screen.key(['['], () => {\n      this.showPresetSaveDialog();\n    });\n\n    this.screen.key([']'], () => {\n      this.cycleToNextPreset();\n    });\n\n    // Theme toggle (Ctrl+T)\n    this.screen.key(['C-t'], () => {\n      this.toggleTheme();\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle command from palette\n   */\n  private handleCommand(cmd: string): void {\n    if (cmd === 'clear') {\n      this.activityStream.clearFilter();\n    } else if (cmd === 'pause') {\n      this.activityStream.togglePause();\n    } else if (cmd === 'refresh') {\n      this.render();\n    } else if (cmd === 'help') {\n      this.toggleHelp();\n    } else if (cmd === 'quit') {\n      this.stop();\n    } else if (cmd === 'heatmap') {\n      this.toggleHeatmapView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'dag') {\n      this.toggleDagView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'replay') {\n      this.toggleReplayView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'errors') {\n      this.toggleErrorsView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'digest') {\n      this.toggleDigestView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'collisions') {\n      this.toggleCollisionsView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'git') {\n      this.toggleGitView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'narrative') {\n      this.toggleNarrativeView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'analytics') {\n      this.toggleAnalyticsView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'budget') {\n      this.toggleBudgetView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'transcript') {\n      this.toggleTranscriptView();\n    } else if (cmd === 'xref') {\n      this.toggleXrefView();\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('filter:worker:')) {\n      const workerId = cmd.replace('filter:worker:', '');\n      this.activityStream.setFilter({ workerId });\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('filter:level:')) {\n      const level = cmd.replace('filter:level:', '');\n      this.activityStream.setFilter({ level });\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('filter:last:')) {\n      const duration = cmd.replace('filter:last:', '');\n      this.handleTimeFilter(duration);\n    } else if (cmd === 'theme' || cmd === 'theme:toggle') {\n      this.toggleTheme();\n    } else if (cmd === 'theme:dark') {\n      getThemeManager().setTheme('dark');\n    } else if (cmd === 'theme:light') {\n      getThemeManager().setTheme('light');\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('preset:load:')) {\n      const presetName = cmd.replace('preset:load:', '');\n      this.loadPresetByName(presetName);\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('preset:delete:')) {\n      const presetName = cmd.replace('preset:delete:', '');\n      this.deletePresetByName(presetName);\n    } else if (cmd === 'preset:save') {\n      this.showPresetSaveDialog();\n    } else if (cmd === 'preset:list') {\n      const presets = this.presetManager.getPresets();\n      if (presets.length === 0) {\n        const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n        this.headerBox.setContent(' {yellow-fg}No presets saved. Use [ to save a preset.{/}');\n        this.screen.render();\n        setTimeout(() => {\n          this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n          this.updateHeader();\n          this.screen.render();\n        }, 2000);\n      } else {\n        const presetList = presets.map(p => p.name).join(', ');\n        const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n        this.headerBox.setContent(` {cyan-fg}Presets: ${presetList}{/}`);\n        this.screen.render();\n        setTimeout(() => {\n          this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n          this.updateHeader();\n          this.screen.render();\n        }, 3000);\n      }\n    } else if (cmd === 'export' || cmd === 'export:file') {\n      this.handleExportToFile();\n    } else if (cmd === 'export:link') {\n      this.handleExportLink();\n    } else if (cmd === 'export:import') {\n      this.handleExportImport();\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('worker:')) {\n      const workerId = cmd.replace('worker:', '').trim();\n      this.handleJumpToWorker(workerId);\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('bead:')) {\n      const beadId = cmd.replace('bead:', '').trim();\n      this.handleShowBeadEvents(beadId);\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('file:')) {\n      const pattern = cmd.replace('file:', '').trim();\n      this.handleShowFileOperations(pattern);\n    } else if (cmd.startsWith('goto:')) {\n      const timestamp = cmd.replace('goto:', '').trim();\n      this.handleJumpToTimestamp(timestamp);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle export to file command\n   */\n  private handleExportToFile(): void {\n    const events = this.store.queryOrdered();\n    if (events.length === 0) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(' {yellow-fg}No events to export{/}');\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 2000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      const { exportToJson, generateExportFilename } = require('../utils/replayExport.js');\n      const fs = require('fs');\n      const path = require('path');\n      const os = require('os');\n\n      const exportData = {\n        version: '1.0',\n        exportedAt: Date.now(),\n        eventCount: events.length,\n        events: events,\n        metadata: {\n          sessionStart: Math.min(...events.map((e: LogEvent) => e.ts)),\n          sessionEnd: Math.max(...events.map((e: LogEvent) => e.ts)),\n          workerCount: new Set(events.map((e: LogEvent) => e.worker)).size,\n        },\n      };\n\n      const filename = `session-${new Date(exportData.metadata.sessionStart).toISOString().split('T')[0]}.fabric-replay`;\n      const exportPath = path.join(os.homedir(), 'Downloads', filename);\n\n      fs.writeFileSync(exportPath, JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2), 'utf-8');\n\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(` {green-fg}Exported ${events.length} events to ${filename}{/}`);\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 3000);\n    } catch (err) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(` {red-fg}Export failed: ${(err as Error).message}{/}`);\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 3000);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle export shareable link command (shows message for TUI)\n   */\n  private handleExportLink(): void {\n    const events = this.store.queryOrdered();\n    if (events.length === 0) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(' {yellow-fg}No events to export{/}');\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 2000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      const { exportToBase64 } = require('../utils/replayExport.js');\n      const base64Data = exportToBase64(events);\n\n      // In TUI mode, we can't copy to clipboard easily, so show the data\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(' {yellow-fg}Shareable link generated (web mode only). See logs.{/}');\n      this.screen.render();\n\n      // Log the shareable URL format\n      console.log('\\n=== Shareable Replay Link ===');\n      console.log('Web UI URL format:');\n      console.log(`https://fabric.example.com/?replay=${base64Data.substring(0, 50)}...`);\n      console.log('Full base64 data logged to ~/.fabric/last-export.txt\\n');\n\n      // Save to file for reference\n      const fs = require('fs');\n      const path = require('path');\n      const os = require('os');\n      const exportPath = path.join(os.homedir(), '.fabric', 'last-export.txt');\n      fs.mkdirSync(path.join(os.homedir(), '.fabric'), { recursive: true });\n      fs.writeFileSync(exportPath, base64Data, 'utf-8');\n\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 3000);\n    } catch (err) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(` {red-fg}Export failed: ${(err as Error).message}{/}`);\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 3000);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle import replay command\n   */\n  private handleExportImport(): void {\n    const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n    this.headerBox.setContent(' {yellow-fg}Import: Use \"fabric replay --file <path>\" CLI command{/}');\n    this.screen.render();\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n      this.updateHeader();\n      this.screen.render();\n    }, 4000);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Parse duration string (e.g., \"5m\", \"1h\", \"30s\") to milliseconds\n   */\n  private parseDuration(duration: string): number | null {\n    const match = duration.match(/^(\\d+)([smh])$/i);\n    if (!match) return null;\n\n    const value = parseInt(match[1], 10);\n    const unit = match[2].toLowerCase();\n\n    switch (unit) {\n      case 's': return value * 1000;\n      case 'm': return value * 60 * 1000;\n      case 'h': return value * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n      default: return null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle time filter command (e.g., \"5m\", \"1h\", \"30s\")\n   */\n  private handleTimeFilter(duration: string): void {\n    const ms = this.parseDuration(duration);\n    if (ms === null) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(` {red-fg}Invalid duration: ${duration}. Use format like 5m, 1h, 30s{/}`);\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 3000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const cutoffTime = Date.now() - ms;\n    this.activityStream.setTimeFilter(cutoffTime);\n\n    const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n    this.headerBox.setContent(` {cyan-fg}Filtered to last ${duration}{/}`);\n    this.screen.render();\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n      this.updateHeader();\n      this.screen.render();\n    }, 2000);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle jump to worker command\n   */\n  private handleJumpToWorker(workerId: string): void {\n    if (!workerId) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(' {red-fg}Worker ID required{/}');\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 2000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Find worker by partial ID match\n    const workers = this.store.getWorkers();\n    const matchedWorker = workers.find(w => w.id.startsWith(workerId) || w.id.includes(workerId));\n\n    if (!matchedWorker) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(` {red-fg}Worker not found: ${workerId}{/}`);\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 2000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Show worker detail panel\n    this.showWorkerDetail(matchedWorker);\n\n    // Set filter to this worker\n    this.activityStream.setFilter({ workerId: matchedWorker.id });\n\n    const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n    this.headerBox.setContent(` {green-fg}Jumped to worker: ${matchedWorker.id.slice(0, 8)}{/}`);\n    this.screen.render();\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n      this.updateHeader();\n      this.screen.render();\n    }, 1500);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle show bead events command\n   */\n  private handleShowBeadEvents(beadId: string): void {\n    if (!beadId) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(' {red-fg}Bead ID required{/}');\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 2000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Set filter to show events for this bead\n    this.activityStream.setFilter({ beadId });\n\n    const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n    this.headerBox.setContent(` {cyan-fg}Showing events for bead: ${beadId}{/}`);\n    this.screen.render();\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n      this.updateHeader();\n      this.screen.render();\n    }, 2000);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle show file operations command\n   */\n  private handleShowFileOperations(pattern: string): void {\n    if (!pattern) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(' {red-fg}File pattern required{/}');\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 2000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Set filter to show events for files matching pattern\n    this.activityStream.setFilter({ filePattern: pattern });\n\n    const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n    this.headerBox.setContent(` {cyan-fg}Showing operations on files matching: ${pattern}{/}`);\n    this.screen.render();\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n      this.updateHeader();\n      this.screen.render();\n    }, 2000);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Parse timestamp string (e.g., \"14:30\", \"14:30:45\", ISO date) to timestamp\n   */\n  private parseTimestamp(timestamp: string): number | null {\n    // Try HH:MM format (assume today)\n    const timeMatch = timestamp.match(/^(\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2})(?::(\\d{2}))?$/);\n    if (timeMatch) {\n      const now = new Date();\n      const hours = parseInt(timeMatch[1], 10);\n      const minutes = parseInt(timeMatch[2], 10);\n      const seconds = timeMatch[3] ? parseInt(timeMatch[3], 10) : 0;\n      const targetDate = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), hours, minutes, seconds);\n      return targetDate.getTime();\n    }\n\n    // Try ISO date string\n    const isoDate = new Date(timestamp);\n    if (!isNaN(isoDate.getTime())) {\n      return isoDate.getTime();\n    }\n\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handle jump to timestamp command\n   */\n  private handleJumpToTimestamp(timestamp: string): void {\n    const ts = this.parseTimestamp(timestamp);\n    if (ts === null) {\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(` {red-fg}Invalid timestamp: ${timestamp}. Use format like 14:30 or 14:30:45{/}`);\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 3000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Navigate to the timestamp in activity stream\n    this.activityStream.scrollToTimestamp(ts);\n\n    const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n    const timeStr = new Date(ts).toLocaleTimeString();\n    this.headerBox.setContent(` {cyan-fg}Jumped to timestamp: ${timeStr}{/}`);\n    this.screen.render();\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n      this.updateHeader();\n      this.screen.render();\n    }, 1500);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Toggle heatmap view\n   */\n  private this.screen.render();\n    } else {\n      this.footerBox.setContent(this.getFooterContent());\n      this.screen.render();\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Show worker detail panel\n   */\n  private this.workerDetail.show();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Toggle worker pin\n   */\n  private toggleWorkerPin(): void {\n    if (this.viewMode !== 'default') return;\n\n    const selected = worker\n      this.pinnedWorkerId = selected.id;\n    }\n\n    this.updateFooter();\n    this.render();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Toggle bead pin\n   */\n  private toggleBeadPin(): void {\n    if    this.render();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Toggle focus mode\n   */\n  private toggleFocusMode(): void {\n    if (this.viewMode !== 'default') return;\n\n    this.focusModeEnabled = this.updateFooter();\n    this.render();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Show preset save dialog\n   */\n  private showPresetSaveDialog(): void {\n    if (this.viewMode !== 'default') return;\n\n    // Create this.presetInputBox.focus();\n\n    this.presetInputBox.on('submit', (name: string) => {\n      this.saveCurrentPreset(name);\n      this.presetInputBox?.destroy();\n      this.presetInputBox = undefined;\n      this.screen.render();\n    });\n\n    this.presetInputBox.on('cancel', () => {\n      this.presetInputBox?.destroy();\n      this.presetInputBox = undefined;\n      this.screen.render();\n    });\n\n    this.screen.render();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Save current focus configuration as a preset\n   */\n  private saveCurrentPreset(name: string): void {\n    if (!name || name.trim() === '') return;\n\n    const pinnedWorkers = this.pinnedWorkerId ? [this.pinnedWorkerId] : [];\n    const pinnedBeads = this.pinnedBeadId ? [this.pinnedBeadId] : [];\n\n    const success = this.presetManager.savePreset(name.trim(), pinnedWorkers, pinnedBeads);\n\n    if (success) {\n      // Show success message briefly\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(` {green-fg}Preset \"${name.trim()}\" saved!{/}`);\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 1500);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Cycle to next preset and apply it\n   */\n  private cycleToNextPreset(): void {\n    if (this.viewMode !== 'default') return;\n\n    const presets = this.presetManager.getPresets();\n    if (presets.length === 0) {\n      // Show message that no presets exist\n      const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n      this.headerBox.setContent(' {yellow-fg}No presets saved. Use [ to save a preset.{/}');\n      this.screen.render();\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n        this.updateHeader();\n        this.screen.render();\n      }, 2000);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Cycle through presets\n    this.currentPresetIndex = (this.currentPresetIndex + 1) % presets.length;\n    const preset = presets[this.currentPresetIndex];\n\n    // Apply the preset\n    this.applyPreset(preset);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Apply a preset to the current focus mode state\n   */\n  private applyPreset(preset: FocusPreset): void {\n    // Enable focus mode\n    this.focusModeEnabled = true;\n\n    // Apply pinned workers (take first if multiple)\n    this.pinnedWorkerId = preset.pinnedWorkers[0];\n    this.pinnedBeadId = preset.pinnedBeads[0];\n\n    // Show message\n    const originalContent = this.headerBox.getContent();\n    this.headerBox.setContent(` {green-fg}Loaded preset: \"${preset.name}\"{/}`);\n    this.screen.render();\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      this.headerBox.setContent(originalContent);\n      this.updateHeader();\n      this.screen.render();\n    }, 1500);\n\n    this.updateFooter();\n    this.render();\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Load a preset by name (for command palette)\n   */\n  private loadPresetByName(name: string): void {\n    const config = label: string; category: string; action: string }> = [];\n\n    for (const preset of presets) {\n      commands.push({\n        label: `Load preset: ${preset.name}`,\n        category: 'Focus */\n  private resizeFileContext(delta: number): void {\n    if (!this.fileContextVisible || this.viewMode !== 'default') return;\n\n    this.splitRatio = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(0.8, this.splitRatio + this.fileContextPanel.getElement().width = `${fileContextWidth}%`;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Toggle help overlay\n   */\n  private toggleHelp(): void {\n    if (this.helpOverlay) {\n left: 'center',\n        width: '50%',\n        height: '55%',\n        label: ' Help ',\n        content: `\nKeyboard Shortcuts\n==================\n\nNavigation:\n  j/k     - Scroll down/up\n  g/G
